Actually, for rows [[1,2],[3,4]], column sums are 1+3=4, 2+4=6, so totals row is \"TOTAL,4,6\".\n\nSo we need to compute column totals and append a row marked as total.\n\nThe `totals.py` has `column_totals` function that takes rows and returns column totals. Maybe `render_rows` should be responsible for rendering including totals if requested.\n\nLooking at `test_render_marks_last_row_as_total`, it expects `render_rows([[1, 2], [3, 4]], mark_last=True)` to return `[\"1,2\", \"TOTAL,3,4\"]`. That doesn't make sense because 3 and 4 are the values of the last row, not the sum. Wait, maybe `mark_last` means prepend \"TOTAL\" to the last row?
